# ​D24.2.70 HFGITR_EL2, Hypervisor Fine-Grained Instruction Trap Register

Source: <https://developer.arm.com/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-70-HFGITR-EL2--Hypervisor-Fine-Grained-Instruction-Trap-Register>

##### D24.2.70 HFGITR\_EL2, Hypervisor Fine-Grained Instruction Trap Register

The HFGITR\_EL2 characteristics are:

Purpose
:   Provides instruction trap controls.

Configuration
:   If EL2 is not implemented, this register is RES0 from EL3.

    This register is present only when FEAT\_FGT is implemented and FEAT\_AA64 is implemented. Otherwise, direct accesses to HFGITR\_EL2 are UNDEFINED.

Attributes
:   HFGITR\_EL2 is a 64-bit register.

###### Field descriptions

![](images/4962-D24.2.70-HFGITR_EL2-Hypervisor-Fine-Grained-Instruction-Trap-Register-img01.svg)

PSBCSYNC, bit [63]
:   When FEAT\_SPEv1p5 is implemented:
    :   Trap execution of PSB CSYNC at EL1 and EL0 using AArch64 to EL2.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     PSBCSYNC
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Execution of PSB CSYNC is not trapped by this mechanism.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, the Effective value of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2">
      HCR_EL2
     </a>
     .{E2H, TGE} is not {1, 1}, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then execution of PSB CSYNC at EL1 and EL0 using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
     <code>
      0x0A
     </code>
     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

        The reset behavior of this field is:

        - On a Warm reset:
          - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
          - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

    Otherwise:
    :   Reserved, RES0.

ATS1E1A, bit [62]
:   When FEAT\_ATS1A is implemented:
    :   Trap execution of [AT S1E1A](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-4-A64-System-instructions-for-address-translation/-C5-4-7-AT-S1E1A--Address-Translate-Stage-1-EL1-Without-Permission-checks?lang=en#reg_aarch64_at_s1e1a) at EL1 using AArch64 to EL2.

     </code>

   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then execution of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-4-A64-System-instructions-for-address-translation/-C5-4-7-AT-S1E1A--Address-Translate-Stage-1-EL1-Without-Permission-checks?lang=en#reg_aarch64_at_s1e1a" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-4-A64-System-instructions-for-address-translation/-C5-4-7-AT-S1E1A--Address-Translate-Stage-1-EL1-Without-Permission-checks?lang=en#reg_aarch64_at_s1e1a">
      AT S1E1A
     </a>
     at EL1 using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
     <code>
      0x18
     </code>
     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.
    </p>

    Otherwise:
    :   Reserved, RES0.

Bit [61]
:   Reserved, RES0.

COSPRCTX, bit [60]
:   When FEAT\_SPECRES2 is implemented:
    :   Trap execution of [COSP RCTX](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-6-A64-System-instructions-for-prediction-restriction/-C5-6-2-COSP-RCTX--Clear-Other-Speculative-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cosp_rctx) at EL1 and EL0 using AArch64 and execution of [COSPRCTX](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-29-COSPRCTX--Clear-Other-Speculative-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch32_cosprctx) at EL0 using AArch32 when EL1 is using AArch64 to EL2.

      COSPRCTX
     </a>
     at EL0 using AArch32 are not trapped by this mechanism.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, the Effective value of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2">
      HCR_EL2
     </a>
     .{E2H, TGE} is not {1, 1}, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>

       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-6-A64-System-instructions-for-prediction-restriction/-C5-6-2-COSP-RCTX--Clear-Other-Speculative-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cosp_rctx" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-6-A64-System-instructions-for-prediction-restriction/-C5-6-2-COSP-RCTX--Clear-Other-Speculative-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cosp_rctx">
        COSP RCTX
       </a>
       at EL1 and EL0 using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
       <code>
        0x18
       </code>
       .
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       Execution of
       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-29-COSPRCTX--Clear-Other-Speculative-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch32_cosprctx" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-29-COSPRCTX--Clear-Other-Speculative-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch32_cosprctx">
        COSPRCTX
       </a>
       at EL0 using AArch32 when EL1 is using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
       <code>
        0x03
       </code>
       .
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

        The reset behavior of this field is:

        - On a Warm reset:
          - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
          - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

    Otherwise:
    :   Reserved, RES0.

nGCSEPP, bit [59]
:   When FEAT\_GCS is implemented:
    :   Trap execution of any of the following AArch64 instructions at EL1 to EL2:

        - `GCSPUSHX`.
        - `GCSPOPCX`.

      SCR_EL3

        The reset behavior of this field is:

        - On a Warm reset:
          - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
          - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

    Otherwise:

nGCSSTR\_EL1, bit [58]
:   When FEAT\_GCS is implemented:
    :   Trap execution of any of the following AArch64 instructions at EL1 to EL2:

        - `GCSSTR`.
        - `GCSSTTR` when [PSTATE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D1-The-AArch64-System-Level-Programmers--Model/-D1-5-Process-state--PSTATE?lang=en#pstate).[UAO](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-2-Special-purpose-registers/-C5-2-30-UAO--User-Access-Override?lang=en#reg_aarch64_uao) is 1.
        - `GCSSTTR` when the Effective value of [HCR\_EL2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2).{NV, NV1} is {1, 1}.

     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3

          - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
          - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

    Otherwise:
    :   Reserved, RES0.

     at EL1 using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value

     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.

  <tr>
   <td>

     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-7-A64-System-instructions-for-the-Branch-Record-Buffer-Extension/-C5-7-1-BRB-IALL--Invalidate-the-Branch-Record-Buffer?lang=en#reg_aarch64_brb_iall" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-7-A64-System-instructions-for-the-Branch-Record-Buffer-Extension/-C5-7-1-BRB-IALL--Invalidate-the-Branch-Record-Buffer?lang=en#reg_aarch64_brb_iall">

DCCVAC, bit [54]
:   Trap execution of any of the following AArch64 instructions at EL1 and EL0 to EL2:

    - [DC CVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-25-DC-CVAC--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cvac).
    - [DC CGVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-7-DC-CGVAC--Clean-of-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgvac), if [FEAT\_MTE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_mte) is implemented.
    - [DC CGDVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-2-DC-CGDVAC--Clean-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgdvac), if [FEAT\_MTE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_mte) is implemented.
    - [DC CVAOC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-27-DC-CVAOC--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-Outer-Cache-level?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cvaoc), if [FEAT\_OCCMO](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-7-The-Armv9-6-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_occmo) is implemented.
    - [DC CGDVAOC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-4-DC-CGDVAOC--Clean-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-Outer-Cache-level?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgdvaoc), if [FEAT\_OCCMO](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-7-The-Armv9-6-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_occmo) is implemented.

    <p>

     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, the Effective value of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2">
      HCR_EL2
     </a>
     .{E2H, TGE} is not {1, 1}, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then if the execution can be trapped, execution at EL1 and EL0 using AArch64 of any of the specified instructions is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
     <code>
      0x18
     </code>
     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    - On a Warm reset:
      - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
      - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

SVC\_EL1, bit [53]
:   Trap execution of `SVC` at EL1 using AArch64 to EL2.

   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">

     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then execution of
     <code>
      SVC
     </code>
     at EL1 using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
     <code>
      0x15
     </code>
     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>

    - On a Warm reset:
      - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
      - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

SVC\_EL0, bit [52]
:   Trap execution of `SVC` at EL0 using AArch64 and execution of `SVC` at EL0 using AArch32 when EL1 is using AArch64 to EL2.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>

 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Execution of
     <code>
      SVC
     </code>
     at EL0 using AArch64 and execution of
     <code>
      SVC
     </code>
     at EL0 using AArch32 are not trapped by this mechanism.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, the Effective value of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2">
      HCR_EL2
     </a>
     .{E2H, TGE} is not {1, 1}, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception:
    </p>
    <ul>
     <li>
      <p>
       Execution of
       <code>
        SVC
       </code>
       at EL0 using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
       <code>
        0x15
       </code>
       .
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       Execution of
       <code>
        SVC
       </code>
       at EL0 using AArch32 when EL1 is using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
       <code>
        0x11
       </code>
       .
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset:
      - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
      - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

ERET, bit [51]
:   Trap execution of any of the following AArch64 instructions at EL1 to EL2:

    - `ERET`.
    - `ERETAA`, if [FEAT\_PAuth](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-4-The-Armv8-3-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_pauth) is implemented.
    - `ERETAB`, if [FEAT\_PAuth](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-4-The-Armv8-3-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_pauth) is implemented.

     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Execution of the specified instructions is not trapped by this mechanism.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then execution at EL1 using AArch64 of any of the specified instructions is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
     <code>
      0x1A
     </code>
     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, [HCR\_EL2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2).API == 0, and this field enables a fine-grained trap on the instruction, then execution at EL1 using AArch64 of `ERETAA` or `ERETAB` instructions is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value `0x1A` with its associated ISS field, as the fine-grained trap has higher priority than the trap enabled by [HCR\_EL2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2).API == 0.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset:
      - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
      - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

CPPRCTX, bit [50]
:   When FEAT\_SPECRES is implemented:
    :   Trap execution of [CPP RCTX](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-6-A64-System-instructions-for-prediction-restriction/-C5-6-3-CPP-RCTX--Cache-Prefetch-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpp_rctx) at EL1 and EL0 using AArch64 and execution of [CPPRCTX](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-34-CPPRCTX--Cache-Prefetch-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch32_cpprctx) at EL0 using AArch32 when EL1 is using AArch64 to EL2.

 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>

 <thead>

  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Execution of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-6-A64-System-instructions-for-prediction-restriction/-C5-6-3-CPP-RCTX--Cache-Prefetch-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpp_rctx" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-6-A64-System-instructions-for-prediction-restriction/-C5-6-3-CPP-RCTX--Cache-Prefetch-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpp_rctx">
      CPP RCTX
     </a>
     at EL1 and EL0 using AArch64 and execution of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-34-CPPRCTX--Cache-Prefetch-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch32_cpprctx" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-34-CPPRCTX--Cache-Prefetch-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch32_cpprctx">
      CPPRCTX
     </a>
     at EL0 using AArch32 are not trapped by this mechanism.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, the Effective value of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2">
      HCR_EL2
     </a>
     .{E2H, TGE} is not {1, 1}, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3

       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-6-A64-System-instructions-for-prediction-restriction/-C5-6-3-CPP-RCTX--Cache-Prefetch-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpp_rctx" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-6-A64-System-instructions-for-prediction-restriction/-C5-6-3-CPP-RCTX--Cache-Prefetch-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch64_cpp_rctx">

       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-34-CPPRCTX--Cache-Prefetch-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch32_cpprctx" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-34-CPPRCTX--Cache-Prefetch-Prediction-Restriction-by-Context?lang=en#reg_aarch32_cpprctx">

        The reset behavior of this field is:

        - On a Warm reset:
          - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
          - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

    Otherwise:
    :   Reserved, RES0.

TLBIVAALE1, bit [47]
:   Trap execution of [TLBI VAALE1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-56-TLBI-VAALE1--TLBI-VAALE1NXS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--Last-level--EL1?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_vaale1) at EL1 using AArch64 to EL2.

    If [FEAT\_XS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-8-The-Armv8-7-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_xs) is implemented and [HCRX\_EL2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-62-HCRX-EL2--Extended-Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcrx_el2).FGTnXS == 0, this field also traps execution of TLBI VAALE1NXS.

    If [FEAT\_XS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-8-The-Armv8-7-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_xs) is implemented and [HCRX\_EL2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-62-HCRX-EL2--Extended-Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcrx_el2).FGTnXS == 0, this field also traps execution of TLBI VALE1NXS.

    If [FEAT\_D128](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-5-The-Armv9-4-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_d128) is implemented, then this field also traps execution of [TLBIP VALE1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-137-TLBIP-VALE1--TLBIP-VALE1NXS--TLB-Invalidate-Pair-by-VA--Last-level--EL1?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbip_vale1).

    If [FEAT\_XS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-8-The-Armv8-7-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_xs) and [FEAT\_D128](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-5-The-Armv9-4-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_d128) are implemented, then this field also traps execution of TLBIP VALE1NXS.

      0b1

   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then execution of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-68-TLBI-VALE1--TLBI-VALE1NXS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--Last-level--EL1?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_vale1" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-68-TLBI-VALE1--TLBI-VALE1NXS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--Last-level--EL1?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_vale1">
      TLBI VALE1
     </a>
     at EL1 using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
     <code>
      0x18
     </code>
     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset:
      - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
      - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

        The reset behavior of this field is:

        - On a Warm reset:

          - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

    Otherwise:
    :   Reserved, RES0.

  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then execution of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-54-TLBI-VAAE1IS--TLBI-VAAE1ISNXS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--EL1--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_vaae1is" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-54-TLBI-VAAE1IS--TLBI-VAAE1ISNXS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--EL1--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_vaae1is">

</table>

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset:
      - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
      - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-58-TLBI-VAALE1OS--TLBI-VAALE1OSNXS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--Last-Level--EL1--Outer-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_vaale1os" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-58-TLBI-VAALE1OS--TLBI-VAALE1OSNXS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--Last-Level--EL1--Outer-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_vaale1os">
      TLBI VAALE1OS
     </a>
     is not trapped by this mechanism.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then execution of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-58-TLBI-VAALE1OS--TLBI-VAALE1OSNXS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--Last-Level--EL1--Outer-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_vaale1os" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-58-TLBI-VAALE1OS--TLBI-VAALE1OSNXS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--Last-Level--EL1--Outer-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_vaale1os">
      TLBI VAALE1OS
     </a>
     at EL1 using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
     <code>
      0x18
     </code>
     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

        The reset behavior of this field is:

        - On a Warm reset:
          - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
          - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

    Otherwise:
    :   Reserved, RES0.

TLBIVALE1OS, bit [22]
:   When FEAT\_TLBIOS is implemented:
    :   Trap execution of [TLBI VALE1OS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-70-TLBI-VALE1OS--TLBI-VALE1OSNXS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--Last-level--EL1--Outer-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_vale1os) at EL1 using AArch64 to EL2.

:   Trap execution of [AT S1E1W](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-4-A64-System-instructions-for-address-translation/-C5-4-10-AT-S1E1W--Address-Translate-Stage-1-EL1-Write?lang=en#reg_aarch64_at_s1e1w) at EL1 using AArch64 to EL2.

 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Execution of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-4-A64-System-instructions-for-address-translation/-C5-4-10-AT-S1E1W--Address-Translate-Stage-1-EL1-Write?lang=en#reg_aarch64_at_s1e1w" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-4-A64-System-instructions-for-address-translation/-C5-4-10-AT-S1E1W--Address-Translate-Stage-1-EL1-Write?lang=en#reg_aarch64_at_s1e1w">

  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>

     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then execution of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-4-A64-System-instructions-for-address-translation/-C5-4-10-AT-S1E1W--Address-Translate-Stage-1-EL1-Write?lang=en#reg_aarch64_at_s1e1w" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-4-A64-System-instructions-for-address-translation/-C5-4-10-AT-S1E1W--Address-Translate-Stage-1-EL1-Write?lang=en#reg_aarch64_at_s1e1w">
      AT S1E1W
     </a>
     at EL1 using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
     <code>
      0x18
     </code>
     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset:
      - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
      - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

DCZVA, bit [11]
:   Trap execution of any of the following AArch64 instructions at EL1 and EL0 to EL2:

    - [DC ZVA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-38-DC-ZVA--Data-Cache-Zero-by-VA?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_zva).
    - [DC GVA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-30-DC-GVA--Data-Cache-set-Allocation-Tag-by-VA?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_gva), if [FEAT\_MTE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_mte) is implemented.
    - [DC GZVA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-31-DC-GZVA--Data-Cache-set-Allocation-Tags-and-Zero-by-VA?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_gzva), if [FEAT\_MTE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_mte) is implemented.

    > #### Note
    >
    > Unlike [HCR\_EL2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2).TDZ, this field has no effect on [DCZID\_EL0](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-43-DCZID-EL0--Data-Cache-Zero-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dczid_el0).DZP.

    <p>
     <code>

  <tr>
   <td>

     <code>
      0b1

   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, the Effective value of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2">
      HCR_EL2
     </a>
     .{E2H, TGE} is not {1, 1}, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then execution at EL1 and EL0 using AArch64 of any of the specified instructions is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
     <code>
      0x18
     </code>
     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

      - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

    - [DC CIGDVAOC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-14-DC-CIGDVAOC--Clean-and-Invalidate-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-Outer-Cache-level?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cigdvaoc), if [FEAT\_OCCMO](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-7-The-Armv9-6-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_occmo) is implemented.

:   Trap execution of any of the following AArch64 instructions at EL1 and EL0 to EL2:

    - [DC CVAP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-28-DC-CVAP--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoP?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cvap).
    - [DC CGVAP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-9-DC-CGVAP--Clean-of-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoP?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgvap), if [FEAT\_MTE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_mte) is implemented.
    - [DC CGDVAP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-5-DC-CGDVAP--Clean-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoP?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgdvap), if [FEAT\_MTE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_mte) is implemented.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     DCCVAP
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Execution of the specified instructions is not trapped by this mechanism.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, the Effective value of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2">
      HCR_EL2
     </a>
     .{E2H, TGE} is not {1, 1}, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then if the execution can be trapped, execution at EL1 and EL0 using AArch64 of any of the specified instructions is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
     <code>
      0x18
     </code>
     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset:
      - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
      - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

:   Trap execution of [IC IVAU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-41-IC-IVAU--Instruction-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ic_ivau) at EL1 and EL0 using AArch64 to EL2.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     ICIVAU
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Execution of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-41-IC-IVAU--Instruction-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ic_ivau" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-41-IC-IVAU--Instruction-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ic_ivau">
      IC IVAU
     </a>
     is not trapped by this mechanism.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL2 is implemented and enabled in the current Security state, the Effective value of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2">
      HCR_EL2
     </a>
     .{E2H, TGE} is not {1, 1}, and either EL3 is not implemented or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .FGTEn == 1, then if the execution can be trapped, execution of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-41-IC-IVAU--Instruction-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ic_ivau" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-41-IC-IVAU--Instruction-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ic_ivau">
      IC IVAU
     </a>
     at EL1 and EL0 using AArch64 is trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value
     <code>
      0x18
     </code>
     , unless the instruction generates a higher priority exception.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset:
      - When the highest implemented Exception level is EL2, this field resets to `'0'`.
      - Otherwise, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

###### Accessing HFGITR\_EL2

Accesses to this register use the following encodings in the System register encoding space:

###### `MRS <Xt>, HFGITR_EL2`

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
  <col/>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     op0
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     op1
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     CRn
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     CRm
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     op2
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b11
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b100
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0001
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0001
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b110
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

```
if !(IsFeatureImplemented(FEAT_FGT) && IsFeatureImplemented(FEAT_AA64)) then
    Undefined();
elsif HaveEL(EL3) && PSTATE.EL == EL2 && EL3SDDUndefPriority() && SCR_EL3().FGTEn == '0' then
    Undefined();
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL0 then
    Undefined();
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL1 then
    if EffectiveHCR_EL2_NVx() IN {'1x1'} then
        X{64}(t) = NVMem(0x1C8);
    elsif EffectiveHCR_EL2_NVx() IN {'xx1'} then
        AArch64_SystemAccessTrap(EL2, 0x18);
    else
        Undefined();
    end;
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL2 then
    if HaveEL(EL3) && SCR_EL3().FGTEn == '0' then
        if EL3SDDUndef() then
            Undefined();
        else
            AArch64_SystemAccessTrap(EL3, 0x18);
        end;
    else
        X{64}(t) = HFGITR_EL2();
    end;
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL3 then
    X{64}(t) = HFGITR_EL2();
end;
```

```
if !(IsFeatureImplemented(FEAT_FGT) && IsFeatureImplemented(FEAT_AA64)) then
    Undefined();
elsif HaveEL(EL3) && PSTATE.EL == EL2 && EL3SDDUndefPriority() && SCR_EL3().FGTEn == '0' then
    Undefined();
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL0 then
    Undefined();
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL1 then
    if EffectiveHCR_EL2_NVx() IN {'1x1'} then
        NVMem(0x1C8) = X{64}(t);
    elsif EffectiveHCR_EL2_NVx() IN {'xx1'} then
        AArch64_SystemAccessTrap(EL2, 0x18);
    else
        Undefined();
    end;
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL2 then
    if HaveEL(EL3) && SCR_EL3().FGTEn == '0' then
        if EL3SDDUndef() then
            Undefined();
        else
            AArch64_SystemAccessTrap(EL3, 0x18);
        end;
    else
        HFGITR_EL2() = X{64}(t);
    end;
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL3 then
    HFGITR_EL2() = X{64}(t);
end;
```
